# Skills · H · part 46

733 entries, humanize to humanizer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **humanize**: Remove AI writing tells from a blog article or social post without changing what it says: not-X-but-Y contrasts, one-line closers, staged openers, forced triads, dashes everywhere, inflated claims, sales language, stock AI words, bold labels and chat leftovers. Matches the site's voice samples when it has them. Use during the HUMANIZE phase of a write or socialcue run, or when the user runs /seoblog:humanize on an existing draft. - **humanize-korean**: Clean up translation-ese and AI-writing tics in Korean prose so it reads like a person wrote it — content (facts, numbers, proper nouns, quotes) stays 100% unchanged, only style and rhythm are touched. Single-pass, done in this session. Triggers — AI 티 없애줘, 번역투 고쳐줘, 한글 자연스럽게 윤문해줘, sounds like a machine translation, remove AI tells from this Korean text, make this Korean read naturally. Not for: marketing copy generation (write it clean the first time — see rules/korean-writing-quality.md), English text, plain spell-check (see korean-spell-check), or book-length manuscripts. - **humanizer**: Remove sinais de escrita gerada por IA de um texto. Use ao editar ou revisar texto pra deixá-lo com som mais natural e humano. Baseado no guia da Wikipedia sobre "sinais de escrita de IA". Detecta e corrige padrões como: simbolismo inflado, linguagem promocional, análises superficiais em "-ando", atribuições vagas, uso excessivo de travessão, regra de três, vocabulário típico de IA, voz passiva, paralelismos negativos e frases de preenchimento. - **humanizer**: Rewrite AI-sounding English so it reads like the writer without changing what it says. Use before saving or sending English prose (docs, PR, issue and commit text, replies, UI copy) and when writing or editing text inside code: test names, assert, error and log messages, code comments, CLI help. Patterns: things acting as subjects, clause pairs joined by semicolons, maxims, not-X-but-Y contrasts, one-line closers, forced triads, dashes everywhere, inflated claims, stock AI words, bold labels, and filler. ([scarf005/claude-config](https://github.com/scarf005/claude-config/tree/HEAD/skills/humanizer))
- **humanizer**: Remove signs of AI-generated writing from text. Use when editing or reviewing text to make it sound more natural and human-written. Based on Wikipedia's comprehensive "Signs of AI writing" guide. Detects and fixes patterns including: inflated symbolism, promotional language, superficial -ing analyses, vague attributions, em dash overuse, rule of three, AI vocabulary words, passive voice, negative parallelisms, and filler phrases. Triggers: user says "insanlaştır", "AI yazısı gibi duruyor", "bu metni doğallaştır", "humanize this", or asks to review/edit text for natural tone before publishing.
